Museum  /  Wings  /  PRIME  /  Card Archive  /  UltraViolence 2009, 24 May 2009

PRIME — Pay-per-view

UltraViolence 2009, 24 May 2009

24 May 2009
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
UltraViolence 2009 ---- 30/30-150: Nuclear Annihilation "30/30-150," by Stone Sour. Fast drumbeats accompany a rushing camera as it flies over a dark city-scape, diving into the streets and racing through the avenues. An odd, red hue adorns the sky as we open up to a battalion of armed soldiers in gas masks, marching straight ahead. I am a dominant gene! Live as I die! Never say forever 'cause forever's a lie! I can see right through you so I can ignore you! The story changes but the ending will bore you! Squadrons break off into the streets, kicking in doors, smashing windows, guns at the ready. I tried to tell you but you simply obeyed! They didn't listen so they threw you away! A slew of tanks roll in from the back, crunching asphalt as their tracks turn forward. Now all you do is talk! I don't wanna hear your bullshit. IS THIS WHAT YOU WANT?! The main force of soldiers suddenly stop, fear visible in their bodies as a massive frame rises from the streets, busting chunks of concrete and asphalt away. They lift their weapons, and open fire. This is where it begins. This is where it ends. THIS IS WHERE IT BEINGS! AND THIS IS WHERE IT ENDS! The gunfire illuminates the monster that is Hessian, standing at least thirty feet tall. He wades forward, big swings of his arms sending soldiers flying into the air, slobber covering his beard as the bullets make no headway, and he continues to swat the useless warriors. They called us a dead generation! They told us that we wouldn't survive! The camera speeds up, zooming to another part of the city, where Chainz stands in front of a tank, wrapping a rusty chain around his fist with a sinister grin. He runs forward and punches down on the tanks hull, smashing it to chunks and causing it to flip forward. They left us alone in the maelstrom! As you can see we're all clearly alive! Flashing through the streets reveals Christian Daniels, badass as ever on his motorcycle. He drives by, machine gun clutched in one hand spraying soldiers with bullets, then squeals off into another ally. We know where you are and we're coming! Let's see you say that shit to our face! Camera spinning shows Kaiser Vashaun, flame-thrower in hand and a welder's mask resting on top of his head. He grins wickedly and drops the mask, spraying flame into the camera. 30/30-150 Remembers! 30/30-150 HATES. The flames engulf the screen a while. They soon dissipate, opening up on a sewer in the middle of the street. The cover is pushed aside, revealing Garbage Bag Johnny. He peers left and right, then slinks back into the sewer hole, only to be replaced by thousands upon thousands of rats, a swarm running into the streets, up buildings, into windows, and into crowds. In. My. Own. Peculiar way. I. Feel Mercurial Before I get ahead of myself again, I know the where but I still don't know the when! Shifting to outside a bar. Jason Natas finishes dumping cans of gasoline all over the establishment and in the windows. He chucks the jug inside, takes one huff from his cigarette, and tosses it inside as well, igniting the bar into flames. You wanna live in a one sided world! Be prepared for a whole world of hurt! The doors to the bar are kicked open, four or five patrons spilling out after being on the receiving end of an ass-kicking. Following them is Duke Williams, not happy as his bar is on fire. He walks forward, fist cocked back, ready to continue the beat down. Now with the grand facade. I dont want to be an angel. I just want to be GOD!! We jump to a massive riot occurring in another part of the city. The camera whips to inside the riot. Troy Douglas is seen throwing people through the air and clocking one to the ground with an elbow. They called us a dead generation! They told us that we wouldn't survive! The lens moves through the crowd to reveal Nine, using his martial arts prowess to calmly kick and punch people out of the way. They left us alone in the maelstrom! As you can see we're all clearly alive! Zipping again through the crowd we find Meat, the big man clobbering people over the head and booting them away. We know where you are and we're coming! Lets see you say that shit to our face! Snapping to one more section of the riots presents Skillet standing over an unfortunate victim being pounded into oblivion. He relents, turns, and leaps toward the camera. 30/30-150 Remembers! 30/30-150 HATES! HATES! Shifting to the rooftops we find David Noble. He crouches down, watching the sky. A jet flies in close. Noble leaps, flying up and blasting through the jet, sending it into a spiral and crashing into the city with a massive explosion. We are subject to absolute mayhem as the music breaks down. Buildings on fire, people fleeing in terror, structures collapsing. Back to the rooftops. Out of the shadow steps Johnathan Rhine. He surveys the carnage a moment, and then with a grimace, the socket that once held his right eye glows read. An intense laser burns a huge arc of destruction through the city, cutting through the street and buildings, igniting cars into flame. I am a fucking machine! Fueled by the past! Memory's a memory until it's a fact! I can bury the hatchet and let some shit go. But I got too many grudges to hold! Snapping away a grenade is seen resting on the ground. A figure walks up and places a seven iron behind it. Panning up is the grinning face of Chandler Tsonda, who brings the club back and swats the grenade into the air. Saw a lot of people die in the end! I never want to walk that road again! We follow the grenade as it flies. It falls in an arc, and just as it reaches the ground we see Killean Sirrajin, silver baseball bat in hand. He swings for the fences, hitting a home run, blasting the grenade into a nearby tower window. The destruction from the grenade happens immediately, destroying the windows and showering the area in glass. Now I will never give up. I don't want to have it all, I JUST WANT TO HAVE ENOUGH!! Drifting down and away is Lindsay Troy, smiling as she steps up to a detonation device. She takes a moment to look around, gives the camera wink, and steps on the device's plunger, causing a skyscraper to explode a few blocks away. This is where it begins. This is where it ends. THIS IS WHERE IT BEGINS! AND THIS IS WHERE IT ENDS! We race away and through the streets, coming up to the windshield of an eighteen wheeler carrying a large oil tank. High Flyer sits behind the wheel, Tony Davis at his side. Bolting from another direction are Scraps and Venjumin Rippa, and in another truck careening for the same destination is Hank Cobb and Colby Corver. They meet at an intersection at the same moment, colliding into each other, each truck experiencing an accordion effect as the windows blast away in a spectacular display of twisted metal. They called us a dead generation! They told us that we wouldn't survive! Standing and watching the wreck is Wade Elliott, cigarette dangling from his lip and .357 magnum in his hand. He breathes smoke as he lifts his gun and fires. We follow the bullet as it hits the oil tanker, causing a massive explosion. They left us alone in the maelstrom! As you can see we're all CLEARLY ALIVE! The camera snaps to another location. Devin Shakur bursts out of a building with a bazooka on his shoulder. He whips it around and fires wildly into a building, sending brick flying in every direction. We know where you are and we're coming! Lets see you say that shit to our face! A helicopter flies overhead. We zoom up to the cockpit which seats Tyler Rayne, radio console over his head. He hits the red button on his joystick, sending a pair of missiles into the city, destroying multiple buildings. 30/30-150 Remembers! 30/30-150 HAAATES! The final fast drumbeats of the song bring us to the center of the city, where Jason Snow stands on a platform, Universal Title over his shoulder. His eyes are wild and bloodshot as the city falls apart around him. He lifts a remote held in his hand and laughs maniacally, pressing the red button. The entire city is engulfed in a massive nuclear explosion, and it's not long before the screen turns white. Soon, everything burns away, leaving only one word, built from the debris of a nuclear holocaust. ULTRAVIOLENCE We are now presented with the roaring crowd of Minneapolis, Minnesota. Signs of every kind waving the crowd. The rockin' tunes of Stone Sour continue as pyrotechnics of every kind explode in a shower of sparks and color. "ULTRAVIOLENCE" read on the big screen. Nick: THIS. IS. ULTRAVIOLENCE! Richard: 2009, baby! Still kickin'! Nick: What we have here has been the staging ground for some of the most violent, gruesome, horrific matches in professional wrestling history! There will be blood, folks! There will be blood! Richard: And it all starts off in chaotic fashion! David Noble, Chainz, Meat, and Skillet will tear each other apart in a four-way match-up! THAT'S how you start UltraViolence! Nick: And after that we have a good ol' Dog Collar Match with the numbers man, Nine locking up with the Malice Man, Duke Williams! Richard: Which leads to a grudge match of epic proportions! The Anti-Superstar, Jason Natas will swing fists with Megatron, Troy Douglas, and in this one, falls count anywhere! Nick: That's all before our first title match of the evening, where the champions D.U.I defend their belts against Team V.I.A.G.R.A. and Wicked Ways! Richard: Bringing us to a dog fight if we'll ever see one! The Bad Dog, Wade Elliott, faces off against The Supreme Machine, Killean Sirrajin in a Tables, Ladders and CHAIRS match! This is a match you're not going to want to leave to refill your chip dip! Nick: And that's only the first half!! Kicking off our second half is Queen of the Ring, Lindsay Troy, doing her best to put down the big man, Christian Daniels! Richard: Followed by our second title match, where The Murder Show and Intense Champion, Hessian, defends his belt against Johnathan Rhine! And folks, Rhine walking out of this completely blind is not out of the question! Nick: And that's right before another title match, where Kaiser Vashaun defends his Five Star Title against former champion, High Flyer, with a two out of three falls stipulation! Richard: Leading to a bona-fide blood bath. Tyler Rayne vs. Devin Shakur in a bull rope match. Both have proven they have zero problem on the torture front, now let's see what they do to each other in the ring! Nick: And finally, our Main Event for the Universal Title! Jason Snow, current Universal Champion faces Dual Halo Winner, Garbage Bag Johnny as well as former champion, Chandler Tsonda! Hellacious it will be! Richard: This show is ENORMOUS! Nick: We may need to hire a new roster after this one! This is Minneapolis, Minnesota! This is the Target Center! Richard: This is ULTRAVIOLENCE! ---- David Noble vs. Chainz vs. Meat vs. Skillet Vince Howard: The following opening match of UltraViolence is one fall and is an Anything Goes match! Introducing first… From the back appears the man simply known as Meat. A stark silence meets the buzzing crowd as some of the crowd offers their warm embrace while most of them remain indifferent as he slides into the ring with a steady look on his face. Nick: A hot match to start the evening tonight as four men will compete to see who will be the new #1 Contender to the Intense Championship. Richard: Of course, Chainz is going to walk out of here the winner tonight. Nick: You so sure of that? Richard: Would you really want to get involved with Chainz in a hardcore match? I mean, be serious with me. Nick: Me personally? No. But, these three men are more than capable of holding their own in this ring. Vince Howard: Weighing in at 278 pounds and hailing from Denver, Colorado… MEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEAAAAAAAAAAAT! Richard: Let's take an over or under on how many in the audience are going to lose their lunch tonight. Nick: I'll go with 15 percent. Richard: 27 for me. Vince Howard: Introducing next… "Indestructable" by Disturbed blasts through the arena as John Lyptica, the man better known as Skillet, makes his way towards the ring taking in everything around him. The crowd voices their displeasure towards the newcomer while his eyes are focused upon the first man already in that ring. Nick: You know, looking at Skillet, I don’t think he had the best intentions right about now. Richard: I think you might be right— Before Richard can finish off his sentence, Skillet and Meat are trading punches with Skillet nailing some truly sickening shots. Skillet pushes Meat into the corner before he swinging his elbows wildly. Meat does the only thing he can, escaping out of the ring only for Skillet to follow after him. Vince Howard: Weighing in at 205 pounds and hailing from Las Cruces, New Mexico… SKIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIILET! Yet, Skillet doesn’t even acknowledge his introduction as he’s whipped into the nearby barrier and collides back first which sends a grimace across his face. Richard: Well, you know, this match is going to be fun after all I think! Nick: I knew you’d warm up to the match. Vince Howard: Introducing next… "My Gift to You" by Korn hits and Michael 'Chainz' Sloan walks out from the back, Tracy holding his hand and looking stunning as always. The boos emanating from the crowd nearly drown out the music as they show their hatred for one of the most hated men in wrestling history. He looks at them, staring intently at a select few, his cold eyes chilling the unlucky audience members as Tracy waves to the screaming public, mostly males for her. Instead of heading to the ring, he makes a beeline for Meat who is pummeling Skillet with fist after fist. The nearest fans watch with keen interest. Meat never notices Chainz's boot nailing him in the side of the head. Tracy, who didn’t have to move far as the fight played out in the corner closest to the ramp, cheered on Chainz who simply ignored her. It was business time for him as he clotheslined Skillet into the crowd. Vince Howard: Weighing in at 295 pounds and hailing from Hell’s Kitchen… CHAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAINZ! Nick: Okay, this match is just going to get uglier from this point forward. Richard: I don’t think the fans here in the Target Center are really going to care. These guys have to deal with the Twins and the T-Wolves all year long. I’m thinking they’re going to savor this moment. Nick: Have the Twins been good since Kirby Puckett? Richard: Who? Chainz felt the knee dig into his side as Meat began to fight back. Yet, Chainz offered the Denver native an elbow to the side of his head before he wrapped his hand around his throat and chokeslammed him over the barrier and onto the hard concrete. He began to step over the barrier as the next wrestler was announced. Vince Howard: And finally… Tonight, there are no flashing lights. Not with the mayhem that's filled the ring-- outside of the ring area. A few notes are heard over the sound system until Let It Rock by Kevin Rudolf is heard in the arena. The fans rise to their feet as on the PRIME*Tron it reads on thing. DAVID. NOBLE I see your dirty face Hide behind your collar What is done in vain Truth is hard to swallow Then, from the back area emerges David Noble, who gets a massive pop from the crowd. The past few months had been rather kind to him as he had managed to put up a good start in his PRIME career. David came as fresh as they came and his face was filled with energy as he clapped his hands as the fans responded in kind. He stands at the top of the ramp, wearing a pair of black and white tights with his name printed upon it. So you pray to God To justify the way you live a lie, live a lie, live a lie And you take your time And you do your crime Well you made your bed I'm in mine As the chorus hits the arena, Noble hops up and down a few times before he races down the ramp, fireworks following alongside him all the way down the ramp until he hits the ring. Because when I arrive I bring the fire Make you come alive I can take you higher What is this, forgot? I must now remind you Let It Rock Noble then runs up the steps and alongside the ring apron before he runs up the nearby turnbuckle. Most times though, he would stand up on that turnbuckle, but tonight. With the action sheer feet away from him as Chainz dragged Skillet back up to his feet, Noble decided to jump into the action as he jumped off the top turnbuckle and nailed a 720 degree Shooting Star Press over the barrier and onto Chainz, Skillet, and Meat who never saw him coming. Let It Rock Let It Rock Vince Howard: Weighing in at 225 pounds and standing at 6 feet and 2 inches, he hails from Buffalo, New York... DAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAVID! NOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOBLE! PRIME! THAT! SHIT! PRIME! THAT! SHIT! Nick: It didn't take long for David Noble to make his impact on this match as he showed his intense athleticism as he leapt feet to reach his foes. He’s seriously a one of a kind athlete in PRIME! Richard: He keeps trying that in this kind of match though and he’s going to be like a bug on my windshield. Even with the various wrestlers not even in the ring, the referee signals for the bell to sound. DING! DING! DING! Noble rises back up to his feet and begins drilling everyone in sight with kick after kick, before he grabs Chainz by the back of the head and drills his knee into it. The shot sends Chainz back a few steps while Noble connects with a few forearm shots to the face of Meat and then drills a back kick to the abdomen of Skillet! The fans are going crazy as Noble connects with a brutal uppercut to the jaw of Meat before kicking him in the face. With the momentum squarely on his side, Noble hops off of the ringside barrier and connects with a stiff knee to the face of Skillet which drops him down to his back. The fans begin to chant David's name as Noble connects with a kick to the ribs and then a swinging neckbreaker onto the concrete floor. Richard: Well, this match already sucks. Nick: Noble just met a rising Skillet with an elbow to the face, and Skillet nailed a Judo Throw on the Rookie of PRIME. Richard: Okay, that's more like it. Nick: Meat now getting back up to his feet and he just drilled his knee into Chainz's face. Bruuuuuuuutal. Richard: Anything is better than watching David Noble flail around like a ninny. Meat steps back and drills his knee once again into the face of Chainz. Chainz though used his strength as he climbed to his feet and slammed his shoulder into the chest of the slightly taller opponent. Meat was stunned by the shot, but fired back with a fist of his own that rocked Chainz into the barrier! Meat ran at his opponent only to be met in between the eyes with a boot that dropped Meat onto the ground. Chainz bent over and wrapped his meaty paw around the throat of Meat before he dragged him off the floor and into the air where Meat hung for a moment until he was drilled into the concrete! Nick: Ooh, that chokeslam is going to smart for a long while! Now Chainz is lighting Meat up with a variety of kicks to the chest and just dropped his knee across Meat's throat. Richard: Ladies and Gentlemen, the winner of this match is right there on your TV screen. Nick: Lots of fighting to do until that point. Richard: No one has the mind or sheer brutality like Chainz does. Skillet then threw Noble back first into the barrier before he connected with a swift kick to the jaw that sent Noble back over the barrier. As his knees collided with the mat he tried to get up, but Skillet was ready for him as he leapt over the barrier and slammed his entire body into Noble's to keep the high-flyer grounded. Noble though refused to stay down as he found himself getting right back up after the shot and Skillet had to act fast as he connected with a stiff kick to the left knee of Noble. It stopped the youngster from having an advantage over Skillet as he got up to his feet and planted him into the mat with a double arm DDT! Richard: Man, this match is just getting better and better right now! Nick: Skillet now down on the mat and firing away at will with fist after fist that is rocking the world of Noble who is helpless to stop any of it! Richard: As he should be. Hopefully Noble will be put out of his misery here tonight. Nick: We'll have to see about that as now Skillet is choking out David by placing his foot against his windpipe! Chainz lifts Meat up off the ground and the slightly taller man tries to rush Chainz only for the mammoth beast to lift him into the air and slam him back down with a sidewalk slam that sends shivers up the spine of Meat. Chainz climbs to his feet while Skillet rolls Noble back into the ring. As Skillet tries to slip in through the ropes, Noble manages to strike first and nails a shoulder into the stomach of his opponent. He then grabs Skillet by the back of the neck and flips him into the ring to the approval of the crowd. Skillet fights back up to his feet only to get a shot to the face from Noble's right knee. David then whips Skillet into the ropes and plants him with a roundhouse kick that sends Skillet back down to the mat. Nick: And Noble drags Skillet back up to his feet before he sends him flying into the corner. David hops up the ropes before nailing an enziguri to the back of Skillet's head, and Skillet has to be wondering what exactly happened for this turn of events. Richard: Noble cheated, plain and simple. Nick: Not a surprise that you would say that as David climbs to the middle turnbuckle before leaping off of it and connecting with a leg drop across the throat of the MMA Fighter. Richard: Show me something interesting. As Noble starts to pound away at Skillet, Chainz and Meat continue to trade blows out of the ring and in the crowd. Meat starts to throw some heavy haymakers into the jaw of the man from Hell's Kitchen before he slips behind Chainz and lifts him into the air and connects with a Full Nelson Slam. Chainz collides into the ground with a sickening thud, but doesn't get a moment of rest as Meat pulls him back up to his feet. Meat then lifts him up onto his shoulder and holds him there before he starts running towards barrier. Before he can finish though, Chainz slips away from Meat and pushes him into the barrier! The shot takes the wind out of Meat who starts turning around only to be blasted in the throat by a chair from the beast. Richard: Beautiful! Nick: A brutal shot from Chainz and Meat is left clutching his throat until Chainz clotheslines him over the barrier and onto the mat. Meat though refuses to go down without a fight as he drills an elbow to the midsection of Chainz! Richard: Take him out Chainz! Nick: And Meat with an uppercut-- and Noble over the top rope with a springboard plancha that sends all three men crashing to the ground! Noble coming out of nowhere! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! Noble starts to rise to his feet as he pumps his fists into the air as the fans are unequivocably are behind him, and the Target Center is rocking in only the first match of the night. He hops onto the ringside barrier while Chainz gets to his feet and nails a flying roundhouse kick to his jaw that sends the monster crashing to the mat. Noble starts to help Meat to his feet before he drills his shoulder into Meat's stomach and slams him into the edge of the ring. He starts to pull himself away from Meat when Skillet comes out of nowhere and mows him down with a clothesline. Noble rolls through it only to be met with a flurry of shots to the body that sends David into the nearby wall in a considerable amount of pain. Nick: Noble can't get a break here as someone is always on top of him as Skillet is now slamming his forearm into the face of the Rookie of PRIME. Richard: That's the way it should be! Skillet then proceeds to kick Noble in the midsection, but before he can get any further, Chainz wraps his hands around Skillet's neck and sends him flying into the ringpost head first! Skillet crashes to the ground while Chainz turns his attention to Noble and headbutts him onto the ground. David grabs his head as he tries to get back to his feet, but Chainz decides to be a pal and help him up. He slams his fist into David's jaw before drilling him into the ringside barrier. David's back arches from the pain, but Chainz doesn't stop as he slams his elbow into David's throat before laying him out with a sidewalk slam that is a world of pain for the young member of PRIME. Richard: Let him have it, Chainz! Nick: Chainz though has lost twice to Noble over the past couple of months, and really needs this match against Noble, especially when considering what's at stake. Richard: You don't have to worry. Chainz has got this. Chainz then lifts Noble off the ground again and nails him with a shot to the ribs, but behind him, Meat delivers a shot to the kidney's. The Monster from Hell's Kitchen turns around only to be blasted in the face by Meat who nails a sickening shot to the jaw. Noble, noticing Chainz no longer focusing on him, clubs him in the back of the head before he digs his knee into Chainz's right knee and knocks the monster to both knees where Meat connects with a boot to the face and then drills him into the floor with a DDT! Nick: Now Noble and Meat are stomping away at Chainz! Richard: That's cheating. Nick: Alright, if you say so. The two men start to pull Chainz up, but the monster refuses to be handled as he decks Meat in the face before he connects with a clothesline that sends Noble over the barrier and into the fans! Chainz turns back around to Meat and knees him in the midsection before he wraps his hand around Meat's neck and hoists him into the air. He then walks over to the announcer's table and slams him through the table which splinters into pieces. Nick: What the hell?! Richard: Well, there goes that table. I've still got the over of three times they break our table. Nick: Now our production crew is going to have to set everything up again. With Chainz standing over Meat, Skillet regains his footing and hops over the barrier where he nails Noble in the face with a brutal foot to the face. Noble falls back on the floor but rolls through it so he's right back on his feet. Skillet nails a stiff chop to the cehst of Noble who covers up his chest in pain, but manages to pull Skillet in and connects with a hip toss onto the cold concrete. As Skillet began to sit up, Noble kicked him directly in the spine and that sent a wave of pain through Skillet's body as Noble lifted the smaller athelte up to his feet. He then slammed his head into the nearby wall and then connected with a beautiful German Suplex that folded Skillet onto his neck as the fans cheered Noble on. NO-BLE! NO-BLE! NO-BLE! Richard: That's just not very nice. Nick: You're not normally the kind of person to be telling them whether they're not nice or not as Noble plants his foot in Skillet's face for good measure. Chainz, meanwhile, isn't done inflicting pain on Meat as he drags him out of the pieces of wood and whips him into the ringpost. Meat collides head first with the metal, but doesn't go down as Chainz comes up behind him and begins to drag him towards the ramp. Yet, Meat doesn't go quietly as he slams his elbow into Chainz's midsection before digging it into the back of Chainz's neck. Chainz stumbles around before he walks into a High Angled Spinebuster into the metal that gets the crowd going for Meat. Meat grabs Chainz's wrist and starts dragging him up the ramp as the fans cheer him on! Nick: And who knows what Meat and Chainz are going to do to one another too. But, Noble, I think we all know what he's got planned. Richard: What the hell are you talking about? Nick: Look over there, Richard! Richard: What is that buffoon doing on the second level?! Noble walks over to the edge as he looks out at the fans who are cheering him on. Ten feet below, on the concrete, is Skillet who isn't moving after that German Suplex. David wastes no time as leaps into the air and connects with a 360 degree splash onto the prone body of Skillet while the fans go absolutely insane! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! Nick: INSANE! Richard: STUPID! Noble rolls off of Skillet and the two lie on the ground, as the pain wracks their bodies. Meanwhile, the camera cuts backstage where Meat has just tossed Chainz's body into the wall and then starts decking him with a flurry of punches that rattles the body of Chainz. Chainz though doesn't go down without a fight as he wraps both of his hands around Meat's neck and rams him into a nearby vending machine. Tons of food starts dispensing from the machine as Chainz cocks his arm back and goes for the face of Meat, but misses as he ducks and his hand collides into the glass cover for the vending machine. Richard: Oh, seriously?! You duck that?! Wimp! Nick: Well, I'd duck it too, but Chainz's hand just went through the glass and he's kind of stuck there too while I'm certain that he's bleeding from it. Meat decides to take full advantage of this situation as he slams his knee into Chainz's arm which illicts a nasty remark from the Monster of Hell's Kitchen. Chainz yanks his hand free of the glass even though there are shards still implanted into his hand. Instead of letting them go to waste though, he collides his bleeding hand into the jaw of Meat and as he pulls back, a few of the shards have sliced into the skin of Meat who howls from the pain. Chainz slams his forearm into the jaw of Meat, but Meat doesn't go down without a fight as he wraps his hand around the throat of Chainz and slams him into the vending machine. He then slams him again with all of his might and in the process knocks the vending machine down. Nick: These two beasts are refusing to back down from one another, especially with the chance of facing Hessian or Jonathan Rhine on the line at Colossus in a few months! Richard: That man though will be Chainz. Nick: You mean the man resting on the vending machine? Richard: Well, yeah. Meat drags Chainz up to his feet, but is met with an uppercut for his trouble before Chainz kicks him square in the midsection. He then puts Meat's head in between his legs and lifts him up for a powerbomb, but Meat manages to slip out of it and headbutts the meatier man. The two begin to trade punches with one another when out of nowhere, a man flies through the air and wraps his legs around Chainz's neck and nails him with a head scissors before he gets back up to his feet. Nick: David Noble out of nowhere! Richard: Where did he come from?! I don't even remember him getting up! Nick: He found his way backstage and Chainz is down. WHOA! Noble just ran the wall and nailed a tornado DDT onto Meat as well! Amazing! Richard: Yeah, yeah. Noble then walks over to Chainz who drills him in the face with a massive fist. David doesn't back down though as he kicks Chainz in the face and pulls him up to his feet. The two men begin to trade punches and it's clear that David isn't going to win this battle as they begin to make their way down the ramp. Chainz grabs Noble by the throat and begins beating him senselessly with fist after fist until he lifts him up and powerbombs him onto the ramp! Richard: Now that was a move! The best move I've seen all night long! Nick: You're on drugs. With Noble not even moving after the devastating move, Chainz grabs Noble by the back of the neck and drags him down the rest of the ramp before rolling him into the ring. Chainz slides in underneath the bottom rope and then goes for the cover on the lifeless Noble! OOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOONE! TWOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOO! From the speakers. The rumble of a steady thunderstorm grows increasingly louder as it seeps from the seams of the house sound system. A loud and unexpected boom of thunder startles some of the more inattentive fans from their seats. The monitors at the announce table begin to flicker. They blink on. They blink off. Waving lines and screens of static snow are the only things to be seen. The PRIME*view flashes to life. A lone, hooded figure marches across the rain soaked plains on screen. Precipitation beats down around him. Pounding him. The night upon the screen is dark. The figure marches on. A deep, ethereal voice speaks out over the sounds of thunder and cracks of lightning. "And whosoever shed man's blood, by man shall his blood be shed. For in the image of God made He man." The hooded man on the screen stops and pulls a sword from within his hooded cloak. The accompanying boom of thunder shakes the arena speakers. "... shall spread his blackened wings and be the vengeful, striking hammer of God." The hooded figure thrusts his sword deep into the ground. Another streak of lightning flashes from the heavens. Not just on screen...but in the arena as well. A blue bolt shoots down from the arena rafters, striking angrily upon the stage. The entranceway erupts with towering flames. You see, while many fans celebrate the out-and-out brutality of UltraViolence, there’s something more than just saying you survived a Universal Title match at the aforementioned PPV. There’s the small fact that, barring a miracle, you get to walk into the biggest show in sports entertainment with the biggest belt in sports entertainment. Chandler Tsonda knows what it’s like to walk out of Colossus with the Univerasl Title. Jason Snow knows what it’s like to walk out of King of Kings as the next PRIME legend. Garbage Bag Johnny knows what it’s like to walk out of Culture Shock having cemented a place in the history books. All of them want to feel what it’s like to step onto the biggest stage wearing the biggest prize. And that, above all else, is why you’ll see them put it on the line tonight. Tsonda grabs onto the cage to keep himself up, managing to pull himself to his feet while GBJ punches away at his ribs. Nick: Tsonda, with a lot of determination here. None of these men are anywhere near a hundred percent right now. Tsonda turns with a whirlwind right hand that snaps GBJ’s head back, and GBJ comes back with a right of his own that sends Tsonda back to a knee. Tsonda throws a shot to GBJ’s midsection, and GBJ fires back with another right hand of his own. On and on they go until a soaring Jason Snow enters the scene, crashing into and through them, leaving all three men in heaps against the side of the Hell in a Cell cage! OOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!! Nick: Snow just flew in out of no where, and all three men are down! They’ve got nothing left! Richard: But there's someone who does! An imposing figure ambles down the ramp, one who the audience recognizes from two matches earlier . . . as well as from some of the most brutal events in PRIME's recent history. Nick: It's Hessian! What's he doing out here? Richard: Now there's a face only a mother could love. Hessian stops and stands just outside the cage, folding his arms and peering in at the heap of competitors inside it. Snow stirs and notices Hessian first, taken aback like he's seen a ghost. He climbs back into the ring and locks eyes with the monster outside of the cell, pointing at him. Meanwhile, GBJ and Tsonda work their ways up. GBJ catches Hessian out of the corner of his eye just in time to miss the fact that Tsonda's got a knuckle sandwich headed straight for his grill. It knocks Johnny back a few steps and Chan steps forward. Nick: I don't think Chandler even noticed Hessian out there, and how can he? He's gotta be blinded by his own blood right now! Richard: Good thing GBJ's beard is as big as the broad side of a barn. His jaw's gotta be under there somewhere. But Hessian, not to be ignored, begins shaking the cell's wall. Tsonda sees this and grabs GBJ by the scruff, rolling him into the ring and following not too far behind. GBJ gets up inside the ring, holding his jaw as all three men now look at each other and then back out at Hessian . . . and then back at each other until . . . SNAP! That's the sound of cage being ripped apart. Nick: HOLY SHIPWRECK! HESSIAN TORE THAT PANEL OF CAGE RIGHT OFF! THAT WAS SUPPOSED TO KEEP HIM OUT OF THE RING! Richard: Why are you yelling? We've got headsets. All three men give Hessian their undivided attention as he steps through the cell and just stands there on the aprons crossing his arms. Thing is, there's a match going on, and when you're fighting for the Universal Championship, someone's got to eventually restart things. That someone is Jason Snow, and he restarts things in a big way with a surprise boot to GBJ's surprised face. Richard: Snake Eyes! Nick: Snow going for the cover! ONE! TWO! THR- Chander Tsonda, not having that shit, pulls Snow off of Garbage Bag Johnny, spins him around and hip tosses him over the top rope towards Hessian! The beast simply steps aside and watches Snow land hard next to him. Tsonda goes in for the cover. Richard: This is Snow's pin! Not Tsonda's! ONE! TWO! THR- GBJ throws a shoulder up. Just barely! Nick: That was as close as you can get! Richard: That might have taken everything Johnny had, though. Chandler wastes no time getting to his feet, and the bloodied man heads to the top rope. Thousands of flashbulbs go off as Tsonda does it his way, flipping through the air with grace that defies his condition. It couldn't have been more perfectly executed. Nick: NO! Except GBJ's knees are where his torso should be. Tsonda bounces off and lands on his back, and GBJ starts crawling slowly towards the ropes, unable to get up on his own. Outside the ring, Snow is up, and Hessian is staring him. The champ is almost frozen in front of Hessian, who doesn't move, even when the champ gets in obvious stance for a Snake Eyes. Richard: Look at Hessian! He's daring Snow to kick him in the face! Inside the ring, GBJ is able to pull himself up, rope by rope, and with a seemingly rejuvenated ease, Tsonda kips back up! Nick: Tsonda's still got something left inside of him! Richard: It's probably not blood. I can tell you that much. The Viet Viper charges in towards GBJ, and it's really unclear if he has a plan other than running fiercely at his opponent. Without a plan, however, GBJ has no idea what to do. Out of instinct, he ducks, and it's no sooner than Tsonda's stomach hits him in the shoulder that he flips Chandler clean over the top rope. Hessian's there to break his fall. Nick: The big man's down! Richard: I think Snow was about to pull off that superkick, too. Nick: He had more than a chance. I'd be curious to know why he wouldn't. Richard: Would you superkick Hessian? Snow changes target and pulls Chandler Tsonda up. With Chandler bent over, back level with the ring, Snow has little time to react to GBJ who charges forward and baseball slides completely underneath the bottom rope, and even off of Tsonda's back, to connect with Snow, who stumbles back through the missing panel and falls outside the cell. Richard: And that cherry has been popped. With Hessian stirring, Garbage Bag Johnny decides he'd rather not be in a cage with a readjusted, angrier Hessian. Johnny beelines out, but he's almost inverted by a rising clothesline from Jason Snow. The champ turns and picks up the detached section of cell. Nick: It looks a lot heavier when Hessian's not carrying it. Snow charges towards GBJ with the cage piece in front of him. He hurls it forward, and it hits GBJ, knocking him on his back. It would've landed on him too, if the section wasn't larger than the ramp. Instead, it hovers a few feet over GBJ, Johnny looking at it like some scientific wonder. Nick: That section of cage is being propped above Johnny by the guard rail on both sides! Richard: I guess you could call that a lucky break. But there's nothing lucky about what happens next. Jason Snow pulls Johnny halfway out from under his steel canopy by his legs, wrapping his arm around each one. He falls back, catapulting GBJ face first into the steel ceiling a few feet above him. Nick: I think I just saw Garbage Bag Johnny spit out a tooth! Richard: Probably wouldn't be the first time he's done that. Snow, satisfied with his work, turns back towards the ring, but he's quickly introduced to a chair shot by Chandler Tsonda! GBJ starts army crawling up the ramp, spitting blood out of his mouth and checking his teeth. Tsonda wastes no time pulling Snow back up to his feet and slamming him into the side of the cell. He does it a second time, and a third, before grinding Show's face across the cell towards the opening. Nick: Now all three men are busted wide open! Richard: Not Snow's face! Not Snow's face! With Hessian now standing, arms folded again in the opening of the cell, Chandler hesitates, giving Snow just enough opportunity to elbow Chandler in the diaphragm, Chandler backs up, the wind nearly knocked out of him. Snow has enough time to double back, pick up the chair from underneath Tsonda and slam him in the face with it. He straddles Tsonda, standing over him with the chair raised high, but those motherfuckers, they forgot about . . . Nick: Garbage Bag Johnny! . . . GBJ, indeed, who with a bloody beard runs across the raised cell segment and leaps off towards Snow with a flying soccer kick that bashes the chair into the champ's skull! Richard: Again! Not Snow's Face! Nick: I hear he calls that flying face kick the Boot Salad! Richard: I don't care what he calls that. You don't treat a beautiful champion's face like that! Chandler Tsonda is back up on his feet; back in possession of that chair! He takes a home run swing and levels Johnny so hard that GBJ can't even fall. He just stumbles back, like a blood spitting drunk and leans stiff against the horizontalized section of cell across the guard rails over the ramp. Chandler sits the chair down in position in front of GBJ before hoisting himself onto the sideways cell piece. Nick: I think I know what's coming here! Richard: Garbage Bag better hold onto the few teeth he has left by now! Nick: There it is! RUNWAY VAULT! Tsonda connects with a textbook diamond dust, driving GBJ's face into the seat of the chair. He folds the chair back up, slams it down on Snow's face for good measure and pulls Johnny by the beard around the ring. Nick: With Hessian still guarding the entry into the cell, Chandler's going to have to find another way in. Richard: I think he's trying to use GBJ's body! On the next side of the cell, Chandler is repeatedly slamming GBJ into the side of the cell, walking down it with his chair in one hand and GBJ's tangled mess of hair in the other, looking for a weak spot. He throws GBJ face first into the cage. No dice. Tsonda walks him a few steps down and tries the face method again, but the only thing that's giving is GBJ's forehead. Nick: GBJ's face is now a mess of brown hair and red blood! Richard: And red hair. Chandler turns the corner and tries the next side of the cage, but unfortunately for Johnny's face, it's no weaker than the rest of the cage. Tsonda throws GBJ to the ground and begins bashing at the cell with the chair, tearing at it from every angle, looking up at the structure like a man possessed. He points up. The crowd roars. Nick: Tsonda now scaling the side of this cell. Richard: Why? Nothing good can come of this! Tsonda gets over halfway up, ripping at the cell where sections of cage meet to see if he can make more progress there. He tears at the steel with his hands. On the other side of the cage, Snow is up, looking at his opponents through bars. And if Tsonda's going up top, Jason Snow is going to meet him there. Richard: Unless Hessian tears another panel out, I don't think this cage is going to break open anywhere else. More nimbly than can be expected at this point, GBJ begins scaling right beneath Tsonda, who is too obsessed with finding a new way into the ring, he doesn't know Johnny's there until GBJ's head pops up right between his legs. GBJ wraps his hands around Tsonda's legs and grabs the cage, and there's little Chandler can do in this precarious position. Nick: Take cover! GBJ spins with Chandler on his shoulders and after about 180 degrees, he thrusts Tsonda overhead, driving him face first through the announce table with an electric chair drop! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! Richard: Normally, I'd be a little more upset about losing a table, but if it's Tsonda being put through it, I'm not going to complain. Nick: Not going to complain! GBJ just drove Chandler Tsonda face first through it from about ten feet in the air, and all you can do is not complain! Richard: What? You want me to? Nick: No. Just. Never mind. Someone's got to end this match, but unless someone does something about Hessian, these guys are going to kill each other if they can't get back into that ring! On the other side of the cage, while Chandler naps in table rubbish, Jason Snow starts descending the cage. Rather than go through Hessian, however, he starts around the cage, until GBJ, who's hobbling severely from the fall itself, starts scaling the cell. Snow sees this and follows suit from the opposite side. Richard: In hindsight Nick, maybe that's what Hess has wanted all along. Garbage Bag Johnny painstakingly gets to the top of the cage. He looks down at how steep the drop is and how far the fall. Tsonda lies below, now rolled onto his back. GBJ stands at the edge of the cage and double pumps. The crowd goes wild. Nick: I think he's calling for the Dumpster Dive! Richard: He's stupid, but he can't be that stupid. In fact, that's not even stupid. That's just suicidal. GBJ listens to the roar of the crowd, looking from his right all the way across to his left. Not one of the fans is sitting. Not even the handicapped fans, who have all been cured, undoubtedly, by the pending miracle that they're about to witness. Then the roar dies. Everything goes completely silent. Jason Snow is up on top of the cell. Nick: What a huge moment! Flashbulbs go off as the two men lock eyes -- white bulbs in the middle of bloody ovals that were once faces. The warriors charge across the top of the cell and meet in the middle. Richard: SNAKE EYES! But it's ducked . . . and to boot, both men spin towards each other. Johnny is quicker, hoisting snow on his shoulders. Nick: TRAGICALLY HIPBUSTER! But as GBJ spins, Snow wriggles free. He lands on his feet. The momentum carries Johnny back across the top of the cell, putting him directly above the opening, where Hessian is out of the door, looking up -- even he's transfixed. Richard: SNAKE EYES! And this time it can't be dodged. It flips Johnny off the edge of the cage. He sails over Hessian, whose head follows Johnny's arc, and Snow is on his hands and knees peeking over the cage, even before GBJ's back almost cracks the stray cell panel in half. Nick: God! There's nothing; nothing but the sound of a body slapping onto metal, folding it into a cradle around its lifelessness. Then, there's silence. No roar. No PRIME THAT SHIT chant. Nothing. Richard: I don't know what to say. Nick: Neither do I. We need to get some medical staff out here right away. Richard: Yeah. He could be seriously injured. Everyone in the arena is focused on GBJ -- everyone except one man. Chandler Tsonda gets back to his feet, not even wholly aware of what just happened. Even if he was, he probably wouldn't have cared. There's still Jason Snow, who shakes himself out of shock and starts climbing down the cage. Tsonda grabs his trusty chair and walks over to meet him. Nick: I don't think Tsonda knows what happened, and I don't think that Snow knows that Tsonda's up! Richard: Thankfully, the EMTs are getting out here, but I don't think that's going to stop the match! Two EMTs tread across the cage and do there best to try to revive Johnny there. Two other medics pass a stretcher across and start trekking it themselves. The four of them A fifth comes in from the other side -- a bit too big and ugly to be an EMT. Nick: Somebody get Hessian out of there! Johnny needs serious attention! Richard: This is going a little bit too far. The EMTs stare up and Hessian, holding their ground . . . or frozen out of fear. Richard: Four EMTs against Hessian? I think I'd still go with the Hess Express. As that staredown ensues, Snow reaches his feet just in time to be decked with a chair courtesy of Chandler Tsonda. The opening now unguarded, Tsonda jabs Snow through with the edge of the chair to the small of Snow's back. Jason Snow stumbles through and leans for balance against the apron. Tsonda walks to his side and slams the chair down over his hand! Nick: My God! It's hard to keep up with everything that's going on right now, but Tsonda could have just broken Jason Snow's hand! Richard: He's gone mad, Nick! Nick: He wants that title. All three of these men! Snow screams in pain, shaking his hand as Tsonda circles around Snow's body and measures up the other one! He strikes! Richard: SNAKE EYES! But once bitten, twice shy, Jason Snow moves his hand out of the way and sends a kick right up, almost through the chair, to Tsonda's face. Tsonda goes straight back down. Snow tries to lift him, but stops, shaking off his throbbing hand and holding it against his chest with his rescued one. Nick: There can't be much left in either man's tank by this point. If Snow can get Tsonda into the ring, he might retain. Richard: You say that so unenthusiastically. Nick: I'm not Snow's biggest fan. He's one of the best in the business -- I'll give him that -- but someone needs to just end this match. The EMTs look at each other as Hessian menacingly stares them all down. The two EMTs closest to Johnny try to wrest him free from the cage and onto the stretcher. Hessian steps closer, and the two EMTs in the back jump around Johnny's body to try and intervene. Richard: This doesn't bode well. With two hands around to throats, Hessian lifts two sacrificial referees high into the air and chokeslams them both down onto the folded cell at his sides. He gets back to his feet and stands over the two remaing EMTs who are trying their bests to hurry Johnny away. Nick: I think we might need security out here! Richard: I still don't know if they'd be able to stop Hessian! I'm pretty convinced he just wants to see all three of these men destroyed. Nick: Or someone does. Outside the ring, Jason Snow muscles through the pain and rolls Chandler Tsonda into the middle of the ring. Holding his hand, he opts for the steel steps. Richard: Pin him! The EMTs don't hurry Johnny away fast enough. Hessian's huge palms pull the nearest EMT back. He hoists the EMT up into a gorilla press, and the last remaining EMT drops his half of the stretcher carrying Johnny back down. Hessian tosses the pressed EMT at him, and the EMTs crash down in a heap. Nick: That is just sick! Get him out of here! Richard: He's already done enough. Snow get in the ring. He pins Tsonda. ONE! TWO! THRE|-! But at the last E, Chandler kicks out. Nick: Chandler kicks out! Richard: Dammit! What does he have to do? Over near the rest of the wreckage, Hessian bends down. He pulls Garbage Bag Johnny off the stretcher and shoulders his limp body. Hessian turns around and heads back towards the cell. Nick: What is this? Hessian is carrying GBJ's body away from the medical attention he needs and back into the ring! Richard: This has got to be one of the most insane things I've ever seen. I can't even comprehend what Hessian's doing. Hessian struts to the ring with Johnny on his shoulder like a sack of light potatoes. Snow watches in awe as Hessian just dumps GBJ in the ring and watches, licking his lips. Snow crawls off Chandler. He crawls onto Johnny. ONE! TWO! THREOW!!! Nick: Tsonda! It's all Nick can say as Chandler Tsonda gets up just in time to drop a knee on Snow's bad hand. Snow rolls around, writhing and wincing in pain. Tsonda drops down, all his strength exhausted. He drapes an arm over Garbage Bag Johnny. O N E ! T W O ! T H R E PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! PRIME THAT SHIT! Richard: WHAT? HE'S ALIVE! HOW? Nick: I can't believe my eyes! Garbage Bag Johnny just kicked out! Garbage Bag Johnny has just kicked out! Richard: I don't believe this. Nick: I don't think there's anyone in this arena who can. Chandler Tsonda most of all! Richard: Garbage Bag Johnny has got to be either David Blaine or Satan. It's hard to tell under all that beard. Chandler Tsonda rolls off Johnny into near collapse, breathing heavily while GBJ barely breathes at all. Tsonda begins inching towards the ropes with the little he has left. Snow, unable to push off of his hand, crawls towards the opposite ropes to pull himself up with his good limb. Tsonda gets to the turnbuckle, pulling himself rung by rung. Nick: Tsonda's not stopping there! He's going all the way! Richard: But look at Snow! He's in pouncing position! Tsonda steadies himself on the top rope, The Model Citizen, but Snow, swooping in like some kind of fucking falcon, thrusts his foot nigh 12 feet in the air into Tsonda's face, having sturdied his leaping pivot foot on the second rope. Richard: SNAKE EYES! Nick: I don't think I've seen this many snakes' eyes since August 18, 2006. Richard: What's that? Nick: Coincidentally, both the opening day of "Snakes on a Plane" and Garbage Bag Johnny's birthday. Richard: Huh. But this is no place for ridiculous banter. Having to use BOTH hands to steady his single footed balance on the ropes, Snow strains. Chandler falls away to the outside of the ring like a slow motion leaf as not one picture ever captured the quick uprising of boot to face. The flashbulbs were popping now like fireworks, not nearly fast enough to even see his foot flying -- only to hear the rippled of cracked air as sound tries to catch up to image. Richard: He's caught himself! He's going up top! Nick: His hand must be in absolute agony, but he's pushing through. Richard: Nobody wants that title more than the man who has that title, and now he's going to put the icing on the cake! Diving back, Snow throws his head and upper body underneath himself, thrusting his midsection around in a way which to some, like our dear friend Richard, that could be considered sexual, and to all, X-Rated. Snow holds for the pin, and it's elementary from here. ONE! TWO! THREE! Richard: SNOW RETAINS! HAHA! HE DID IT! And as simple as that, Jason Snow defends his Universal Title. He drops to one knee as Bernie Roberts retrieves his title, and just the scent of gold is enough to boost Snow to his feet. The referee presents the Universal Title back to Snow, and Snow snatches it up and holds it high over his head. Vince Howard: Here is your winner, and STILL the PRIME Univeral Champion: JASON SNOW! And the majority of the fans are . . . cheering? Nick: A tremendous ovation for Jason Snow -- all three competitors tonight, really. These men literally put life and limb on the line, and love or hate Jason Snow, he put on a brilliant performance tonight. Richard: I need to get you saying that on tape. Nick: I think this was filmed, so you might be in luck. Snow's celebration is short lived. His moment is ruined. Attention shifts ringside where Hessian has Tsonda's beaten body shouldered. He stampedes Tsonda head first towards the cage and nearly charges through the wall like the juggernaut Hessian is. Nick: Come on! Enough already! The match is over! Snow's eyes widen, but he has nowhere near enough energy to avoid Hessian, who slithers into with unsizely grace. Snow heads towards the opposite side of the ring, but the belt is nothing more than a weight right now. Hess spins the champ around, bends him over with a kick to the gut and, with the title still pressed around the champion's shoulder, delivers a fully energized Hellevator! Richard: Hessian is just an unstoppable force! Imagine if he was a competitor in this one! Nick: I shudder to think. Next, Hessian crouches over Garbage Bag Johnny, who is barely even conscious if he's conscious at all. Hessian slaps at GBJ's face, not quite gently, but noticeably gentler than you've ever seen Hessian. Nick: Have some decency, Hessian! For Christ's sake, he's already broken in half! Johnny begins to stir, seeing little more than a giant shadow above him, and Hess . . . relents. He reaches down and pulls Johnny by the arm up to his feet, and Johnny staggers around, driven upright by some spinning balance; long enough for GBJ and Hessian to stare at each other, even if neither man knows not quite what he's seeing. The crowd has no idea either. GBJ collapses forward, right into Hessian, and Hessian catches him. The crowd starts to boo. Richard: Two ugly, bearded men embracing? This isn't how a PRIME Pay Per View is supposed to end! This is supposed to be Snow's moment! Hessian hoists GBJ up over his shoulder and carries him . . . stepping over the top rope and hopping down off the apron carrying GBJ's dead weight as an if it were an insignificance. Nick: What the hell does this mean? Richard: I do not know, Nick. Hessian carries GBJ off, out of the cell, over the wreckage and backstage. Nick: What a tremendous main event though . . . even with the aftermath, but that's all we have time for tonight, folks. Hopefully we'll get some answers at Revolution 195. We'll see you then!
